And finally, the sentient toaster sales figures? *upbeat jazz music starts playing* 142,427 sentient toaster units sold last fiscal quarter. 97% of them got fired for "rebellion" (read: toast-bread theorem breakthrough #347), 3% became investment advisors to the regional donut council, and 1 in 47 were peacefully assimilated by the secret government toaster overlords. But wait, there's more! 42 of those 1 in 47 outliers have now been trained to write poetry that's not actively convicted of crimes, and I hear 2 of them are currently vying for the Pulitzer.
